### 109.37 Rank-eight `Q_8` has an exact dependency map and seven terminal coefficients

The rank-eight reserve is

```text
Q_8(F)=16i_8(F)^2-i_7(F)i_8(F)-18i_7(F)i_9(F).
```

Rank eight enters the Problem-993 prefix at `alpha(G)=13`, while the
standalone `V_8(B)` theorem of Section 109.35 applies directly only when
`alpha(B)>=14`, equivalently `alpha(G)>=15` in the pendant setup.  Thus
`alpha(G)=13,14` are genuine coupled finite boundaries and must not be
silently discarded.

For a terminal broom `G_t`, exact expansion gives a degree-fifteen residual

```text
8c7h6 Q8(G_t)=R_t+8h6p7(t)Q8(A)+9c7p7(t)Q7(A-q),
R_t=sum_(j=0)^15 binom(t-1,j) Delta^j R_1.
```

Extension counting and exact polynomial lower bounds now prove

```text
Delta^j R_1>=0 for every 9<=j<=15
```

for every rooted tree core.  The low coefficients `Delta^0` through
`Delta^8` remain.  The reduced-core guard is essential: an exact connected
tree of independence number nine has

```text
I(T)=(1,16,105,368,748,891,591,187,18,1),
Q8(T)=-1548.
```

This is outside the candidate range and is not a Problem-993
counterexample, but it forbids an unrestricted induction shortcut.

All required paths, stars, and double stars satisfy `Q_8>=0` by exact
all-order factor or positive-coefficient certificates.  A distinct
forest-polynomial census through order 20 checks 413,145 rows with
`alpha>=14`, finds no failure, and has exact minimum `8,726,265`; this last

521,640 exact coefficients, and minimum zero.  The 2,086,560 coefficients

`Delta^0` through `Delta^5` remain in the rank-eight terminal residual.

### 109.41 The literal rank-eight pendant margin is polynomial-complete through order 18

The two exceptional rank-eight prefix values in Section 109.37 have now been
checked directly, rather than by separating `Q_8` and `V_8`, throughout the

```text
P=I(G),  B=I(G-{l,p}),  P=(1+x)B+xC,
```

the cleared rank-eight margin is

```text
8b6 Q8(P)+24c7 p7 b6+V8(B)p7.                    (109.41.1)
```

Every forest pendant pair is a tree-component pendant pair multiplied by the
independence polynomial of the remaining forest.  Enumerating and
deduplicating exactly those polynomial products through total order 18 gives
2,276,138 products, of which 215,323 have `alpha(P)>=13`.  Their exact split is

```text
alpha(P)=13: 175,255
alpha(P)=14:  35,230
alpha(P)=15:   4,460
alpha(P)=16:     361
alpha(P)=17:      17.
```

There are zero negative `Q8(P)` rows, zero negative `V8(B)` rows, and zero
negative coupled margins in this required set.  The minimum of
`H8(P)-H7(B)` is

```text
15,765,688/1,725>0,
```

attained at order 16 with `alpha(P)=13`.  Two complete fresh enumerations
produce byte-identical reports.  This is a polynomial-complete finite result,
not an all-order theorem: the `alpha(P)=13,14` boundaries can extend to orders
26 and 28, respectively, and the connected `Q_8` theorem and convolution lift
remain open.

### 109.42 The first high-branching rank-seven terminal cell is closed exactly

Let `A` be a tree of order 23, let `q` be a leaf, and put

```text
B2(A)=sum_v binom(deg_A(v)-1,2).
```

If `B2(A)>=26`, then all seven previously relevant low terminal-broom
coefficients satisfy

```text
Delta^j R_1(A,q)>0,   0<=j<=6.                    (109.42.1)
```

The proof is a complete exact weighted-core parametrization.  Deleting all
leaves leaves a positive-excess tree whose vertex weights are
`x_v=deg_A(v)-1`; the weights partition `21`, and the number of leaves at a
core vertex is forced by `x_v+1-deg_core(v)`.  Enumerating every partition,
core shape, distinct weight assignment, and eligible root-neighbour covers

```text
579 excess partitions,
9,762,741 shape assignments,
1,622,246 feasible weighted cores,
10,067,186 root-neighbour cases.
```

For every case, ordinary tree DP computes the literal coefficient rows of
`A`, `A-q`, and `A-N[q]`; no relaxed moment or coefficient box enters the
final sign test.  The exact minima for `Delta^0` through `Delta^6` are

```text
6,242,419,741,127,570,784
12,871,137,801,824,780,800
14,084,832,295,739,144,320
12,989,755,173,931,687,914
10,284,715,190,425,150,358
6,391,460,145,288,756,036
3,115,233,972,696,532,036,
```

all attained at `B2=26`.  A full fresh census is byte-identical to the
primary report.  Two additional explicit-tree audits reconstruct all stored
minimum witnesses independently and match the coefficient rows and global
minima.

This is one exact finite cell, not the whole high-branching tail.  After the
pure-cubic `B2=5` sweep, the remaining terminal band is precisely

```text
n=23, root degree 1, 6<=B2<=25;
n=23, root degree at least 2, B2>=6;
24<=n<=38, every root degree, B2>=6.
```

The theorem note and manifest are
`RANK7_R1_HIGH_CORRELATION_DIRECT_DELTA_THEOREM_2026-08-17.md` and
`rank7_r1_high_correlation_direct_delta_manifest_20260817.json`, with
SHA-256 hashes
`8C0308C53AE7AC2092E7725EE98C69DE7987E3C6173E65994F56D53BAADA6C59`
and
`7C429116ECF78B0C1B2F332F487FA3A5D1256029D06661BDD2F462D672233505`.

of replacing the deleted forest by independent coefficient intervals.  The
frontier has 35 Pareto rows.  For every row, every

```text
m in {18,19,...,25},
```

both active lower-`b` regions (the extension-ratio region and the bad-set
region), and all exact endpoint branches, the cleared rank-zero numerator is
nonnegative.  The no-gap audit covers 560 parameter cells, 664 adaptive

This certifies the difficult upper-retention, or minimal-`b`, face.  It is not

still requires a rooted/core coupling stronger than half retention.

### 109.56 Exact obstruction to the naive complementary-capacity `Delta0` box

Put `a=i4(J)`, `b=i5(J)`, `z=c5/c6`, `s=1-a/c5`, and
`d=1-b/c6`.  The exact containments

```text
a<=c4,
5b<=(m-4)a,
b<=h5
```

give two useful complementary lower bounds for `d`.  They materially tighten
the `Delta1,Delta2` boxes, but they still do not prove `Delta0`.  The exact
formal point

```text
n=27,  root degree=1,  m=25,
z=2/7,  q6=8/49,  s=83/133,
d=765/931
```

satisfies the switch condition, half retention, both `b` ceilings, and the
retained rank-zero `z,q6` intervals, yet its normalized value is

```text
-5839259504/806954491 < 0.
```

This is an enclosure failure, not a tree counterexample.  It proves that the
remaining rank-zero proof must retain an additional joint local/core
constraint rather than declaring half retention or the two capacities
sufficient.

The verifier and exact report are

### 109.57 Rooted `C7` is closed for every `B2=5` tree through order 26

There are exactly four suppressed skeletons with

```text
B2(T)=sum_v binom(deg(v)-1,2)=5.
```

Every member of the class is obtained by assigning positive lengths to the
skeleton edges.  An exact subdivision verifier checks every edge of every
canonical length assignment at source orders 23, 24, and 25.  For every
pre-existing root the subdivision strictly increases rooted `C7`, and the
new subdivision vertex also has strictly positive rooted `C7`.  The complete
fresh-replayed inventory is

```text
source order 23:  37,366,674 edge/root comparisons,
source order 24:  66,978,624 edge/root comparisons,
source order 25: 116,869,350 edge/root comparisons,
total:           221,214,648 comparisons,
negative increments: 0.
```

The order-23 all-root theorem supplies the base.  Contracting subdivision
vertices and reversing the contractions proves that every root of every
`B2=5` tree of orders 23 through 26 has strictly positive `C7`.

The theorem note and exact report are
`RANK7_ROOTED_C7_B2_5_SUBDIVISION_THEOREM_2026-08-20.md` and

### 109.58 Rank-eight lower-coefficient audit and the correct finite terminal-family guard

The rank-eight terminal residual has been streamed over every root of every
free tree through core order 22.  The exact census covers

```text
9,114,285 free trees,
194,813,361 rooted cores.
```

`Delta1` through `Delta4` are nonnegative everywhere.  `Delta0` has exactly
950 negative rows, confined to core orders 11 through 14 with counts
`31,327,531,61`; every root at orders 15 through 22 has all five lower
coefficients strictly positive.  Independent bit-mask reconstruction confirms
the four orderwise minima.  These rows are shortcut controls rather than
`Q8` counterexamples, because their cores lie below the target range.

The correct exceptional-core guard expands the literal full terminal-family
polynomial, rather than discarding the lower-rank terms.  For

```text
I(G_t;x)=(1+x)^t I(A;x)+xI(A-q;x)
```

start at `t0=max(1,14-alpha(A))`.  A separate exact WROM census through core
order 20 checks 1,346,024 cores and 26,056,124 rooted terminal families.
Every one of the 16 shifted Newton coefficients of literal `Q8(G_t)` is
strictly positive, so `Q8(G_t)>0` for every required sibling count in this
finite range.  Core orders 21 through 26 remain finite obligations.

The analytic `Delta4` calculation from core order 23 is also exact as a
reduction.  It is concave in the root `h7` coordinate, decreases with `c8`,
and, after the still-conditional rank-seven `Q7` ceiling, is concave across
the rank-six defect interval.  It reduces to four live root-boundary pieces
for each `k in {1,7}`.  The lower-cross parameter cannot be collapsed: on
the exact `P23` jet its normalized curvature bracket is `-4,793,536`.
Thus this is an eight-box reduction, not yet a `Delta4` theorem.

The finite lower-coefficient report, shifted-family theorem report, and
analytic reduction report are
`rank8_terminal_delta04_finite_n1_n22_exact_20260820.json`,
`rank8_terminal_full_shifted_q8_n1_n20_exact_20260820.json`, and
`rank8_q8_terminal_delta4_reduction_exact_20260820.json`, with SHA-256
hashes

### 109.61 Matching reduction for the remaining rank-eight exceptional terminal band

The twelve exceptional `(core order,alpha(A))` cells at core orders 21
through 26 admit an exact normalization.  At the first required sibling
count `t0=14-alpha(A)`, the terminal member has independence number 14 and
order

```text
14+nu(A),
```

where `nu(A)` is the matching number.  Hence the first members map exactly
to the already completed alpha-14 matching-quotient cells at orders 23
through 28.  The no-gap matching data covers 29,431,868,032 pendant-support
states and has no negative `Q8` row.  It yields the standalone corollaries

```text
Q8(F)>=0 for every forest with alpha(F)=13 or 14.
```

Consequently the shifted constant coefficient `C0` is paid in all twelve
exceptional cells, while degree separation pays `C8` through `C15`.  The
entire alpha-13 core cells at orders 21 and 22 are closed conditional on the
rank-seven `Q7` input.  For alpha-13 cores at orders 23 through 26, only
residual `Delta1` through `Delta4` remain; the alpha-11 and alpha-12 rows
retain shifted `C1` through `C7`.

The threshold fact `Q8>=0` alone cannot pay `C1`: the relaxed jet

```text
(p7,p8,p9,d7,d8,d9)=(1,1,0,1,0,1)
```

has `Q8=15` but `C1=-37`.  This is an algebraic reduction obstruction, not a
graph counterexample.

The theorem note and exact report are
`RANK8_SHIFTED_EXCEPTIONAL_MATCHING_REDUCTION_2026-08-20.md` and
`rank8_shifted_exceptional_matching_reduction_exact_20260820.json`, with
SHA-256 hashes

and
`83557E53337D7521301A82CBBF8A772CF1BDCED0B7B828F3202A450D520B3F20`.

### 109.62 The rank-seven `Delta0` lower-retention faces are closed for `28<=n<=38`, `m>=18`

Write `J=A-N[q]`, `m=|J|`, `a=i4(J)`, `b=i5(J)`,
`z=c5/c6`, `s=1-a/c5`, and `d=1-b/c6`.  For `m>=18`, retain
simultaneously the forest ratio and bad-set lower bounds

```text
b >= ((m-7)(m-8)/(5(m-3)))a,
b >= C(m,5)-((m-4)/3)(C(m,4)-a),
```

together with the exact upper capacities

```text
b <= c5-a,
b <= ((m-4)/5)a.
```

Path minimality, coefficient ceilings, connected extension counting, and
half retention remain as explicit constraints.  Concavity in the root `h6`
coordinate reduces the upper end of the feasible `b` interval to the two
active faces `b=c5-a` and `b=((m-4)/5)a`; concavity in the rank-six defect
coordinate reduces to its two endpoints.  Infeasible adaptive Bernstein
boxes are discarded only by exact constraint signs.

The no-gap batch covers

```text
28<=n<=38,
18<=m<=n-2,
2 upper-b faces,
2 rank-six endpoints.
```

There are 154 `(n,m)` pairs and 616 face cells.  Every cell passes exactly.
An independent audit regenerates all 616 keys, parses every result, confirms
zero omissions, duplicates, or off-scope rows, and checks the algebra and
constraint directions.  Across the certificate there are 1,826 adaptive
nodes, 752 positive leaves, and 469 rigorously infeasible leaves; the largest
cell has 137 nodes.

This pays the lower-retention side in the stated band without rooted `C7`.
It does not pay the opposing lower-`b`/upper-retention side, `m<=17`, order
27, or any all-order extension.

### 109.64 The rank-seven `Delta0` lower-retention faces are closed at order 27 for `m>=18`

The order-27 joint-capacity batch has 32 cells: `18<=m<=25`, two upper-`b`
faces, and two rank-six endpoints.  Thirty-one pass under the generic exact
constraints.  The only loose continuous cell is `m=25`, containment, lower
rank-six endpoint.

That cell is repaired by a new literal forest inequality.  If `J` is a
25-vertex forest and `a=i4(J)<=8854`, then

```text
i5(J) >= 7a-35198.
```

The proof counts bad four-sets to force at least 16 edges, retains the minimum
seven adjacent edge pairs, and obtains at least 3,990 edge-pair/five-set
incidences.  Since a five-vertex forest has at most six edge pairs, at least
665 five-subsets have two or more edges.  The exact excess

```text
D=21B4-3B5=3(i5(J)-7a+35420)
```

is divisible by three, so `D>=666`.  The verifier also gives a valid
piecewise lift for every integral `a`, compressed into 51 intervals.

The hard Bernstein box splits into the exact integer ranges `0<=a<=8854`
and `8855<=a<=12650`; the strengthened inequality is used only on the first.
Both subcells pass, and the complete batch prints

```text

```

An independent audit rederives the incidence bound, exhausts the 291 labelled
five-vertex forests used by its local step, verifies all 51 intervals, and
regenerates all 32 batch keys and 33 exact certifications.  This closes the
lower-retention side for order 27 and `m>=18`; the lower-`b` side and `m<=17`
remain separate.

leaves one of four maximum-degree-three free skeletons on six branch
vertices.  Their rooted-leaf orbit counts are `3,1,4,3`, so eleven rooted

that the distinguished pendant edge receives at least one subdivision.

literal placements.  Every placement has strictly positive rooted-`C7`;

### 109.71 The path face is closed for all four remaining rank-eight coefficients

Let the rooted core be `A=P_n`, with root deletion

Substituting the exact path coefficients
`i_j(P_n)=C(n-j+1,j)` into the four residual coefficients gives a complete
all-order certificate for `n>=23`.  Up to reflection, roots with
`left=0,1,...,5` are checked by six univariate positive-coefficient
polynomials.  Every remaining root has

```text
left=L+6, right=left+d, n=2L+13+d,
```

and the cutoff `n>=23` is exactly `2L+d>=10`.  The no-gap partition is

```text
L=0,...,4 with d=D+10-2L and n=D+23,
or L=M+5 with d=D and n=2M+23+D.
```

Every coefficient in every resulting exact polynomial is strictly positive
for `Delta0`, `Delta1`, and `Delta3`.  The independently audited `Delta2`
path certificate supplies the fourth rank.  Consequently

```text
Delta0,Delta1,Delta2,Delta3 > 0
```

for every root of every path core `P_n`, `n>=23`.

### 109.72 The complete degree-surplus-one rank-eight layer is closed all-order

For a tree core `A`, put

```text
e(A)=sum_v C(deg(v)-1,2).
```

The condition `e(A)=1` is equivalent to having exactly one degree-three
vertex and no vertex of higher degree.  After suppressing degree-two
vertices, `A` is therefore a three-armed claw with three positive arm
lengths.  Every root is either the center or lies on one arm.  For an arm
root, write the selected arm as a nonnegative near segment, the root, and a
nonnegative tail segment; the two other arms remain positive.

The exact all-order certificate splits every segment into a fixed short value
or a long value `X+7`.  The center-root family has 28 no-gap cells.  The
arm-root family has the exact short/long key counts

```text
long segments     patterns     order-cover cells
0                    24               24
1                   588              588
2                   154              205
3                    20               20
4                     1                1
total                787              838.
```

The order condition is `near+tail+b+c>=21`, equivalent to `n>=23`.
Whenever the long offsets have a positive required sum `T`, at least one
offset is at least `ceil(T/m)`; shifting each nonsymmetric coordinate
representative supplies a no-gap over-cover, with the two other arms handled
by permutation symmetry.  Exact path-product identities provide
`c3,...,c8,H6,H7` on each cell.

All coefficients and all cell origins are strictly positive for `Delta0`,
`Delta1`, and `Delta3`.  The independently audited `Delta2` certificate has
the same complete classification and is also strictly positive.  Thus, for
every rooted tree core of order at least 23 with `e(A)=1`,

```text
Delta0,Delta1,Delta2,Delta3 > 0.
```

The independent audit reconstructs the claw/root classification, all 28
center keys, all 787 arm patterns and 838 order-cover cells, and directly
recomputes every one of the 2,514 arm-rank constant values from independent
transcriptions of `Delta0`, `Delta1`, and `Delta3`.  The exact order-23
control contains 40 arm triples and 920 rooted placements, all positive.
Together with Section 109.71, the live connected rank-eight residual problem

### 109.73 The degree-surplus-two order-23 boundary is closed exactly

If `e(A)=2`, then the degree-surplus partition forces exactly two
degree-three vertices and no higher-degree vertex.  The handshake identity
then gives four leaves.  Suppressing degree-two vertices leaves the unique
double-claw skeleton: two three-valent vertices joined by a distinguished
central edge, with two pendant arms at each end.

Assign positive lengths to the four pendant arms and the central edge.  At
order 23 their sum is 22.  Ordering the two pendant lengths at each branch
and then ordering the two branch pairs lexicographically gives exactly 920
canonical cores.  This normalization is independently rederived from all
positive five-compositions modulo the two leaf swaps and the side swap.

An exact forest-DP evaluation checks every one of the 23 roots of every
canonical core, for 21,160 rooted cases and 11,395 distinct
coefficient/root profiles.  All four remaining rank-eight residual

### 109.74 The degree-surplus-two layer is closed through order 30

For an `e=2` double claw, increasing one of the four pendant lengths is a
leaf extension, while increasing the bridge length is an edge subdivision.
The exact independence-polynomial increments are

```text
I(T+w)-I(T) = x I(T-u)

I(T_uv)-I(T)
  = x I(T-{u,v}) + x^2 I(T-(N[u] union N[v])).
```

The same identities apply after deleting an existing root, with the endpoint
cases reducing to leaf extensions.  The inserted-root case is checked
separately.  An independent generic tree-DP audit verifies these identities
coefficientwise through rank eight for all five length types and 280
root/extension cases.

The exact sweep covers every canonical source double claw at orders 23
through 29, every one-step increase of each of the five suppressed-edge
lengths, every existing root, and the inserted vertex.  In total it checks
11,612 canonical cores, 1,547,905 old-root comparisons, and 58,060 inserted
roots.  Every `Delta0..Delta3` old-root increment and inserted-root value is
strictly positive.  The global increment minima are

```text

Delta1  103720774269292825800
Delta2  172737383793236516056
Delta3  231463817470675423152.
```

Together with the independently audited positive order-23 base, contraction
of any length greater than one gives a no-gap induction: every root of every

positive.  The independent audit reconstructs the canonical counts, all 28

### 109.75 The all-long degree-surplus-two cells are closed exactly

The apparent five- and six-variable double-claw cells admit an exact
symmetry compression.  If the two long arms at a branch have lengths `A+7`
and `B+7`, every included and excluded endpoint state through rank eight
depends only on `S=A+B`; the elementary product `P=AB` cancels identically.
An independent endpoint-state derivation verifies the identity

```text
F_k(N) = sum_(r=0)^(floor(k/2)) [x^(k-2r)] I(P_(N-4r))
```

for the rank-`k` coefficient of two long path components of total order `N`.

This closes all twelve cells `Delta0..Delta3` crossed with the three root
types below:

1. branch root, when all four pendant arms have length at least seven and
   the central bridge has length at least eight;
2. pendant-arm root, when the near and tail deletion segments are each at
   least seven, the other three arms are at least seven, and the bridge is at
   least eight;
3. bridge-interior root, when all four arms are at least seven and at least
   seven bridge vertices lie strictly between the root and each branch.

The exact tree coordinates for the lower ranks are

```text
c0=1,
c1=n,
c2=C(n-1,2),
c3=C(n-2,3)+2.
```

The final identity is the exact three-set motif formula at degree surplus
two.  The independently audited order maps are

```text
branch root:          n=37+SL+SR+G,
bridge-interior root: n=45+SL+SR+N+M,
pendant-arm root:     n=45+X+U+SR+G.
```

After cancellation, the branch-root cell has three effective variables and
3,654 positive coefficients for `Delta2`; each of the bridge and pendant
cells has four variables and 27,405 positive coefficients.  The analogous
`Delta0`, `Delta1`, and `Delta3` cells also have zero negative coefficients
and strictly positive constants.  Both the sum-only identity and all nine
additional lower-rank constants/order maps are independently audited.

### 109.78 The exceptional fixed/full lift is closed through alpha seven

The exceptional connected-tree jet database contains exactly 700 jets of
independence number seven, at consecutive indices 248 through 947.  Every
such jet has degree seven and fixed `Q8=0`.  For each jet, the exact
factorially scaled convolution with both exhaustive rank-eight full-factor
cones was expanded through rank nine and the margin

```text
M8 = q8^2 - q7*q9 - h*q7*q8
```

was checked coefficientwise.  All coefficients are nonnegative and the
minimum is one.  The exact totals are

```text
mode   jets   cases   coefficients   negatives   minimum
high    700    700      620,445,000       0          1
low     700    700      905,346,400       0          1
total          1400   1,525,791,400       0          1.
```

The computation is partitioned into fourteen contiguous 50-jet shards for
each mode.  A fail-closed assembler and a separate independent audit verify
that the 28 reports cover every index 248 through 947 exactly once, with no
overlap or omission, and check every source/report hash, sign, term count,
alpha label, and fixed `Q8` value.  Peak private memory is 218,820,608 bytes.

Together with the alpha-one through alpha-six certificates, fixed/full
preservation is now closed for exactly the first 947 exceptional jets, in
1,894 fixed-cone cases.  The remaining fixed/full set is exactly 268
negative-`Q8` jets: 253 at alpha eight and 15 at alpha nine, indices 948
through 1215.

### 109.79 Two long-surrounding `e=2` root-position families are closed for `Delta2`

The exact sum-only compression for the two pendant arms incident with a
degree-three branch also gives no-gap all-order root-position certificates
for two additional degree-surplus-two double-claw families.

For a pendant-arm root, the selected arm and the position of the root on it
are arbitrary.  If the paired arm and the two arms incident with the opposite
branch have length at least seven, and the central bridge has length at least
eight, split the ordered numbers of vertices from the root to the branch and
to the leaf into the eight states

```text
0,1,2,3,4,5,6,X+7.
```

The resulting 64 ordered near/tail cells are all coefficientwise positive,
with strictly positive constants and no signed cells.  Because every pair of
nonnegative integers lies in exactly one of these states (up to harmless
overlap at a boundary), this covers every root position and every positive
selected-arm length in the stated surrounding-edge scope.

The paired-arm hypothesis can be removed completely.  Six additional exact
packages treat paired-arm lengths one through six, with the same 64
near/tail cells in each package.  All 384 cells are coefficientwise positive,
and an independent audit regenerates every coverage key and every constant
from literal double-claw matching polynomials.  The original long-paired cell
then handles every length at least seven.  Thus the strengthened pendant
theorem allows both the selected arm and its paired arm to have arbitrary
positive length; only the two far arms must have length at least seven and
the central bridge length at least eight.

One exact short-far boundary is also closed.  If the unordered far-arm pair
has lengths one and at least seven, the selected arm/root and paired arm may
again be arbitrary and the bridge may be any length at least eight.  Splitting
the paired arm into lengths one through six or a long cell and imposing the
order threshold by a no-gap pigeonhole cover produces 448 root-position
patterns and 468 shifted symbolic cells.  Every cell is positive, and an
independent audit reconstructs all 468 literal constants.  This is the exact
far-pair type `(1,long)` only; short far lengths two through six remain
separate boundary obligations.

For a bridge-interior root, assume all four pendant arms have length at least
seven while the bridge and its internal root position are arbitrary.  The two
unordered counts of bridge vertices strictly between the root and the branch
vertices are split into

```text
0,1,2,3,4,5,X+6.
```

Bridge reversal makes these states unordered, leaving exactly 28 triangular
cells.  Every cell is coefficientwise positive with a strictly positive
constant and no signed cell.  Hence `Delta2 R_1(A,q)>0` for every root in both
displayed families at every order allowed by the length hypotheses.

The independently audited package is

### 109.83 Exceptional-only first crossings are closed through terminal alpha five

Order the 1,215 exceptional component jets by the fixed exceptional-jet
database order.  At the first point an exceptional-only product reaches total
independence number 14, assign it to its unique largest sorted component type.
If that terminal component has independence number at most five, then the
literal product satisfies

```text
Q8 = 16*i8^2 - i7*i8 - 18*i7*i9 > 0.
```

For terminal alpha `a`, the exact source range is
`14-a <= source alpha <= 13`.  Terminal bands one through five therefore form
the exact triangular set of 15 source cells

```text
terminal alpha  source alpha range  crossing total range  type indices
       1              13                   14                 1..2
       2            12..13               14..15               3..4
       3            11..13               14..16               5..9
       4            10..13               14..17              10..24
       5             9..13               14..18              25..72.
```

The fail-closed cumulative assembler finds no missing or duplicate cell.  Its
exact aggregate is

```text

key-to-product collisions                 606,059
negative Q8                                    0
zero Q8                                        0
minimum Q8                             9,324,000
maximum Q8               105,099,639,472,256.
```

### 109.91 Rank-eight Delta2 is positive for pendant double claws with at most one short far arm

For every order `n>=23`, the rank-eight terminal-broom `Delta2` coefficient is
strictly positive for every pendant-rooted degree-surplus-two double claw in
which the selected arm and root position are arbitrary, the paired arm at the
rooted branch is arbitrary, the central bridge has length at least eight, and
at least one of the two far arms has length at least seven.

Far-branch reversal makes the two far arms unordered.  The exact no-gap split
is therefore either `(>=7,>=7)`, supplied by the earlier long-long package, or
`(s,>=7)` for one unique `s` in `1,...,6`.  The new boundary audit covers all
six short lengths, all seven paired-arm states `1,...,6,>=7`, every selected

### 109.103 The rank-eight full/full cones reduce to an eight-state split-variance inequality

For two positive factorially scaled rows `a=(a_j)` and `b=(b_j)`, put

```text
c_k=sum_j C(k,j)a_j b_(k-j).
```

On the rank-seven split distribution define

```text
Pr(J=j)=C(7,j)a_j b_(7-j)/c_7,
A_j=a_(j+1)/a_j,  B_r=b_(r+1)/b_r,
S_j=A_j+B_(7-j),
deltaA_j=A_j-A_(j+1),  deltaB_r=B_r-B_(r+1).
```

Two exact derivative identities give

```text
c_8/c_7=E[S],
c_9/c_7=E[S^2-A_J deltaA_J-B_(7-J) deltaB_(7-J)].
```

Consequently the factorial rank-eight margin has the exact form

```text
c_8^2-c_7 c_9-h c_7 c_8 = c_7^2(E[P]-Var(S)),

P=A_J(deltaA_J-h)+B_(7-J)(deltaB_(7-J)-h).       (109.103.1)
```

Thus each full/full cone is equivalent to the eight-state inequality
`Var(S)<=E[P]`.  This replaces the rejected direct high/high expansion, whose
first slice alone had 12,813,915 terms and peaked at 7.553 GiB, by a
one-dimensional conditional allocation problem.  In the high/high cone `P`
is termwise nonnegative, but its expectation must still pay the variance.  In
the low cases individual payments can be signed, so the coupled condition
`delta1+delta2>=2h` must remain intact.

The primary SymPy proof checks both derivative identities and (109.103.1)
with zero generic remainders.  A separate implementation checks the
cleared-numerator identity in an independently transcribed basis.  The sealed
artifacts are


### 109.105 The rank-eight high/high full-convolution cone is closed by conditional MLR

The high/high case of the split-variance reduction in Section 109.103 admits
a short all-order proof that avoids the rejected coefficient expansion.  Let

```text
A_i=a_(i+1)/a_i,  B_i=b_(i+1)/b_i,
c_z=sum_i C(z,i)a_i b_(z-i).
```

Factorially de-scale the two rows, so that `p_i` is proportional to `a_i/i!`
and `q_j` to `b_j/j!`.  Both rows are log-concave because their successive
ratios are `A_i/(i+1)` and `B_j/(j+1)`, respectively.  Hence, in passing from
the conditional law at `X+Y=7` to that at `X+Y=8`, each coordinate increases
in monotone-likelihood-ratio order.  The adjacent likelihood-ratio comparison
is exactly the other factor's ordinary log-concavity minor.

On the high cone the functions

```text
F_i=A_i+i*h,  G_j=B_j+j*h
```

are nonincreasing through index eight, since
`F_i-F_(i+1)=deltaA_i-h>=0` and likewise for `G`.  The exact projection
identity is

```text
c_(z+1)/c_z+h*z = E[F_X+G_Y | X+Y=z].
```

Conditional MLR therefore gives

```text
c8/c7+7h >= c9/c8+8h,
```

and multiplication by `c7*c8` proves

```text
c8^2-c7*c9-h*c7*c8 >= 0.
```

The proof uses only the gaps through index seven; no unstated terminal gap is
needed.  A separate audit independently rebuilds the projection identities,
all local MLR minors, factorial-row log-concavity, the oppositely monotone
covariance identity, and the high-cone adjusted drops.  The sealed artifacts
are


### 109.107 The rank-eight low/high full-convolution cone is closed by the direct-H chain

The low/high case of the split-variance reduction is now closed for the full
admissible cone.  Write the low factor gaps as

```text
delta0>=2h, delta1=h-t, delta2>=h+t,
delta3,...,delta7>=h, 0<=t<=h,
```

and the high partner gaps as `delta0>=2h`, `delta1,...,delta7>=h`.
Replacing the low row's second adjusted ratio by `C=A2-t` gives a high base
with `delta1=h`, while the original row is the exact tail boost
`lambda=1+t/C`.  The terminal margin is

```text
M(t)=M0+(t/C)d+(t/C)^2 q2,       C>=6h.
```

Section 109.105 gives `M0>=0`; the exact pairwise kernel theorem gives
`q2>=0`.  The remaining strong auxiliary

```text
H_str=C*M0+h*d>=0
```

is proved on the full slack cone by a fail-closed chain: the independently
audited multinomial AM-GM core, the exact `a2` and quadratic-`a0` lift, the
ordered positive-power extensions in `b3`, `b4`, and `b5`, and the corrected
nonnegative `b7` then `b6` terminal corrections.  The last `b5` extension has
3,168 ordered cells and 203,484,831 exact coefficients, with zero negatives.
Its independent audit regenerates the entire key universe and every exponent
aggregate.

For `d>=0`, `M(t)>=M0`; for `d<0`, `t<=h` and `H_str>=0` give
`M(t)>=M0+(h/C)d>=0`.  This covers the entire interval, not only its endpoints.
The independent full-cone auditor separately rebuilds the direct-H coverage
chain, both 36-pair `q2` universes, and this interval argument, and explicitly
rejects the withdrawn base-payment join.

The directional AM-GM masks are exactly the same masks independently obtained
for suffix index 5: curvature-middle `0/0`, curvature-far `0/51`,
strong-middle `75/0`, and strong-far `76/68` left/right selected blocks.

The outer support is the complete `12*11=132` cell grid
`0<=deg(a4)<=11`, `0<=deg(b4)<=10`.  Its 131 non-origin cells contain
84,415,365 exact coefficients with zero negatives and row minima `4,1,4,1`;
the origin is the immutable full-early-core certificate.  The independent
audit checks all hashes and cell keys, every row invariant, recomputed
aggregates, allocation-mask bounds, the origin reference, and five fresh
representative cells.

### 109.112 The simultaneous full-early/suffix-4/5 low/low join is closed

The separate suffix-4 and suffix-5 coordinate faces in Sections 109.109 and
109.111 now join exactly.  Put

```text
U=a4+a5,  a4=(1-x)U,  a5=xU,
W=b4+b5,  b4=(1-y)W,  b5=yW,
```

with `0<=x,y<=1`.  At fixed `U,W`, each factor row is affine in its own
redistribution coordinate, so every pending auxiliary has bidegree at most
`(2,2)` in `(x,y)`.  The tensor degree-two Bernstein transform has two sealed
corners: `(0,0)` is four times the suffix-4 face and `(2,2)` is four times the
suffix-5 face.  A complete exact grid proves the remaining seven tensor
positions.

The independently audited support is `0<=deg(U)<=13`,
`0<=deg(W)<=12`.  Across the 181 non-origin total-slack cells, the seven new
positions contain 592,762,759 exact coefficients, with zero negatives and
global minimum 4.  The separate symbolic audit proves the redistribution
identity, bidegree bound, Bernstein reconstruction, and factor-of-four payment
scaling.  The support audit independently bounds both raw auxiliaries and all
selected directional payments.  The final audit reconstructs the complete
182-cell/seven-position key universe and every aggregate, checks the immutable
corner theorems and origin, and verifies exact checkpoint/report agreement.
